## Abandoned Stadium Fashion Poster

> Sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Cinematic editorial poster. The person from the reference photo, same face, same hairstyle, same appearance. {argument name="outfit" default="Oversized black zip-up hoodie with gothic white lettering on sleeves, black baggy wide-leg pants"}. Pose: sitting relaxed in worn red stadium seat, legs spread wide, one arm draped over backrest, other hand resting on knee. Chin slightly down, cold direct stare into camera. Low angle shot, camera at knee level shooting upward. Background: {argument name="setting" default="abandoned concrete stadium interior"}, rows of worn faded red plastic seats, thick white atmospheric smoke drifting low. Heavily dented rusted {argument name="vehicle" default="white Nissan Skyline R33"} suspended mid- air on thick industrial chains, red taillights glowing through smoke. Plain dark bare concrete wall, no graffiti no text on wall. Lighting: cinematic low-key, deep shadows, red practical glow from taillights, cool desaturated palette
```

## Fashion Campaign Triptych Layout

> Lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A vibrant, high-end studio promotional image featuring the same stylish young Japanese woman repeated across three side-by-side panels in a horizontal triptych layout. She must clearly appear as the same person in all three panels (consistent face, hair, skin tone, identity), with strong face consistency.
Each panel shows her fully from head to mid-thigh (no cropping), centered and clearly visible.
The character is a fashionable, modern Japanese girl with a trendy, stylish appearance, natural glowing skin, well-groomed hair, and a confident personality.
Each panel presents a different stylish pose, angle, and expression:

Left panel: wearing a {argument name="outfit 1" default="chic mustard yellow polka-dot dress with sunglasses"}, playful and confident pose, slightly tilted body, hand in hair, looking off-camera with attitude.

Center panel: wearing a {argument name="outfit 2" default="colorful patterned headscarf, fitted red top, and elegant beige high-waisted pants"}, facing forward with a confident, calm smile, strong eye contact, fashion model posture.

Right panel: wearing a {argument name="outfit 3" default="modern green sweater with light-toned pants"}, relaxed yet stylish pose, slight side angle, soft natural smile, hands casually positioned.
Background: three bold gradient panels (orange, purple, blue), each featuring soft abstract curved shapes and subtle cloud textures, giving a premium modern advertising aesthetic.

Lighting: bright, soft, evenly diffused studio lighting, vibrant color grading, high contrast, ultra-sharp details, realistic skin tones.

Style: luxury fashion campaign, modern commercial photography, editorial magazine quality, highly detailed, cinematic polish.
Strict requirements: same Japanese girl in all panels, stylish fashion-focused appearance, different poses and angles in each panel, no text, no logos, no typography, no stickers, no branding elements, no cropping of characters, full composition visible, perfectly balanced horizontal layout.
```

## Sunlit Ladder Lifestyle Shot

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic 8K photorealistic cinematic editorial image, 4:5. A young woman sitting on top of a red step ladder outdoors under a vivid blue sky with scattered white clouds. Full-body framing, subject clearly visible including legs and shoes. Pose: seated on ladder, body upright. Orientation: facing LEFT. Head tilt: slightly tilted upward. Gaze: upward. Expression: calm, relaxed, eyes closed or softly shut.
Hairstyle MUST match the model reference: long, dark, flowing hair with soft waves, neatly styled with natural volume and clean parting.
Outfit MUST match the model reference: {argument name="top" default="beige knit sweater with horizontal stripe details"}, {argument name="pants" default="fitted brown pants"}, casual sneakers.
Lighting MUST match: bright natural sunlight, strong directional light casting natural shadows. Background: open sky with clouds, no additional elements.
Replace the model’s face completely with the provided user face, preserving exact facial identity, proportions, beauty features, makeup, and expression from the user image. Blend seamlessly with lighting and angle. Do not retain any of the original model’s facial features.
Camera angle: slightly low angle looking up. Maintain original composition and framing exactly. No creative changes.
```

The lipstick tube is encrusted with brilliant, shimmering diamonds on its base and sits atop a plush, circular velvet ottoman. A woman in a sparkling, floor-length silver evening gown leans gracefully against the oversized cosmetic, holding a standard-sized version of the same lipstick.
​The background exudes regal opulence, featuring towering marble arches, draped {argument name="curtain color" default="lilac"} silk curtains, and massive crystal chandeliers that bathe the room in a soft, ethereal light. In the distance, several figures in formal attire and suits of armor stand as silent guardians of the hall, adding to the theme of royal sophistication. The text at the top reads "AM COSMETICS Est. 2024," while the bottom features the elegant script: "Elevate your beauty. The Diamond Collection." The overall atmosphere is one of surreal luxury and timeless glamour.
```

## Neo-Noir Editorial Collage

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A 2x2 grid of cinematic, hyper-realistic editorial portraits of a {argument name="subject" default="young women"}(keep the facial features unchanged as uploaded image)with a sharp jawline and dark, textured hair. The aesthetic is {argument name="style" default="Neo-Noir dark fantasy"}, blending high-fashion streetwear with surreal, symbolic elements. The color palette is strictly limited to {argument name="colors" default="deep crimson, obsidian black, and crisp white"}.
Quadrants & Details

Top Left: Close-up portrait. The man has a dark, thorny crown across his eyes with a single
blood-red rose covering one eye. He exhales a thick, volumetric plume of cigarette smoke. White ash-like particles are scattered across the shoulder of his black wool coat.

Top Right: A surreal double-exposure style portrait. His face is framed by two glowing, out-of-focus candle flames in the foreground. He has intense, slightly glowing reddish-amber eyes.

Bottom Left: Full-body shot. He stands in a minimalist white studio space, wearing an oversized black trench coat and wide-leg trousers. He confidently rests one foot on a glowing, intricate glass chandelier placed on the floor, surrounded by thousands of fallen red rose petals.

Bottom Right: Waist-up portrait against a deep red gradient background. He holds a large bouquet of dark red roses. Fine white snow or ash falls softly through the air, caught by high-speed photography.
Technical & Lighting

Lighting: Masterful chiaroscuro and rim lighting. High-contrast shadows define the facial structure and the premium textures of the heavy wool coat and silk burgundy tie.

Camera: Shot on Arri Alexa 65 with an 85mm f/1.2 lens for supreme clarity and buttery bokeh.

Rendering: 16k resolution, Unreal Engine 5.4, ray-traced reflections on the glass chandelier, subsurface scattering on the skin, and hyper-detailed fabric weaves.

Atmosphere: Moody, avant-garde, "Vogue" editorial, dark romanticism.
```

## Underwater Chinese Ink Dreamscape Portrait

> Sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A stunning, highly realistic portrait of a {argument name="subject" default="woman"} based on the original model (the facial structure, hairstyle, and person must be exactly 100% identical to the reference image — no changes allowed) submerged in an ethereal underwater dreamscape infused with traditional Chinese ink painting aesthetics. A cinematic, surreal portrait of a young Asian woman suspended gracefully in crystal-clear water, her body caught in a moment of weightless elegance. Her long black hair flows and spirals around her like living calligraphy brushstrokes, intertwining with streams of black ink that bleed and bloom through the water in mesmerizing organic patterns. Ancient Chinese ink paintings come alive around her—{argument name="ink elements" default="majestic dragons, koi fish, blooming lotus flowers, and misty mountain landscapes"} materialize from the swirling ink, some rendered in traditional monochrome, others bursting with touches of vermillion red and imperial gold. These illustrations seem to dance and breathe, wrapping around her form like protective spirits. Her eyes are open, gazing directly at the viewer with an expression of serene power and ancient knowing—a goddess caught between worlds. Her lips are painted in classic Chinese opera red, a striking contrast against her porcelain skin. Delicate ink marks flow across her body like living tattoos—poetry fragments, phoenix feathers, and peony petals painted directly onto her skin by invisible brushes. She wears a flowing hanfu-inspired garment in sheer white silk that billows and undulates in the water like clouds, revealing glimpses of her silhouette beneath. Golden hairpins and jade ornaments drift weightlessly around her head, suspended in liquid time. Red silk ribbons spiral upward from her wrists and ankles, creating dynamic leading lines through the composition. Bioluminescent particles float around her like scattered stardust, adding points of cyan and soft pink light that illuminate her features from unexpected angles. Air bubbles rise slowly past her face, each one containing tiny reflections of the ink artwork surrounding her. The color palette blends traditional ink wash tones—deep blacks, subtle grays, and pure whites—with unexpected accents of cinnabar red, turquoise, and molten gold. The overall mood is hauntingly beautiful, mythologically powerful, and impossibly dreamlike. Photographed with underwater photography techniques, ultra-high resolution, dramatic chiaroscuro lighting from above creating god-rays through the water surface, with selective focus keeping her face and expression razor-sharp while the ink formations create painterly soft edges throughout the frame.{argument name="aspect ratio" default="9:16"}
```

## Premium Studio Ad Visual for Starbucks Frappuccino

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Act as a senior art director and studio photographer for high-end advertising campaigns and e-commerce.

Format & Canvas
Vertical 9 :16. Background: {argument name="background color" default="warm off-white (# F4EFE6)"} or a neutral tone characteristic of the brand, with no visible shadows.

Background Pattern
Apply the brand’s signature motif as a large, tone-on-tone watermark (10–18% opacity). Motif size should be 15–25% of the canvas width, with a soft, slightly embossed feel.

Logo Placement
Position the Starbucks logo centered in the upper third. Width: 40–50% of the canvas. Use a flat, two-dimensional rendering in official brand colors.
Place the wordmark below the logo in spaced uppercase letters, matching the logo color. Wordmark height: approximately 60% of the logo height.

Product Placement
Photorealistic Starbucks frappuccino with full material fidelity (cup, lid, straw, textures, branding details).
Position: bottom right, angled at 25–35°. Cropped at 60–75%, occupying 55–65% of the canvas.
Use soft contact shadows (8–12% opacity), with no harsh shadows.

Lighting
Soft, directional daylight (5500–6000K). Key light from top left, fill from the right.
Minimal contrast (1.2–1.5:1), with global illumination for subtle light bounce.
No edge highlights; reflections only on metallic elements. Avoid hard shadows.

Typography & Finishing
At the bottom: a thin horizontal line (1 px, 80% width, in brand color).
Below it: microcopy ({argument name="microcopy text" default="social link or collection tag"}) in a clean sans-serif font, aligned with the context.

Technical Specifications
Photorealistic CGI (KeyShot/Octane style), 9: 16, ultra-high detail, f/16 depth of field, sharp edges, neutral color accuracy, slightly warm background, no environmental reflections.
Mood reference: official visuals from Louis Vuitton / Bottega Veneta / Celine.
```

Use soft ivory or cool champagne neutral background (no warm gold tones). Same minimal structure as DAY 1 with elevated contrast and sharper visual presence. Cocktail: one espresso martini or dark-toned cocktail in a refined glass. Style shift from soft romantic beach to confident night city energy, mood bold, sharp, magnetic, modern, high-fashion. Aspect ratio 4:5, render style luxury_editorial_collage_realism, sharpness ultra_crisp, color grade midnight_black_chrome_silver. Negative prompt: gold typography, warm tones, yellow dress, beach vibe, extra accessories, messy layout, low contrast, cheap styling, low detail. Also create a matching realistic fashion scene using the reference woman as the exact subject, keep her face, identity, and proportions consistent, transform into DAY 2 night luxury fashion setting, single realistic cinematic scene not collage. Environment: luxury rooftop or elegant city night setting with soft lights, modern atmosphere, mood confident, modern, magnetic, night luxury. All outfit, accessories, and styling must naturally match the reference woman's appearance, proportions, and aesthetic. Outfit: high-fashion evening outfit that suits the reference woman perfectly, modern, elegant, slightly bold, night-appropriate. Bag placed naturally on a table or next to her, never floating. Shoes worn by the woman, fully integrated with the outfit. Jewelry worn on the woman, subtle but luxury, not separate objects. Drink either in her hand or placed naturally on a table, luxury cocktail matching the night setting. No floating objects, no random scatter, realistic positions only, all objects must obey real-world placement and balance. Camera: medium or full-body fashion shot, editorial slightly cinematic, lens 50mm or 85mm, focus sharp on face and outfit. Lighting: night luxury lighting, soft highlights with subtle reflections, clean modern contrast. Must have: same reference woman, identity preserved, cohesive styling, realistic placement, no floating objects, night luxury mood, single scene no collage. Negative: collage, floating bag, floating shoes, floating jewelry, random objects, bad placement, different face, identity change, messy composition, low realism.

[{argument name="Product Type" default="PRODUCT TYPE"}] (Clothing / Food / Automotive / Tech).

Goal: Generate a professional "scrapbook" style collage poster for [{argument name="Brand Name" default="BRAND NAME"}] centered around a [{argument name="Product Type" default="PRODUCT TYPE"}].

1. HERO PRODUCT (BOTTOM FOCUS)

- Object: A high-definition, realistic 3D render of a [{argument name="Product Type" default="PRODUCT TYPE"}] by [{argument name="Brand Name" default="BRAND NAME"}] at the bottom center.

- Placement: Slightly angled to show depth, with soft realistic contact shadows on a plain off-white floor.

2. THE MULTI-LAYERED COLLAGE STACK

- Photos: A vertical stack of overlapping photos with RAGGED, TORN paper edges rising above the product.

- Content: Photos should show [{argument name="Product Type" default="PRODUCT TYPE"}] in use, lifestyle shots related to [{argument name="Brand Name" default="BRAND NAME"}], and close-ups of specific details.

- Materials: Physical swatches of textures related to [{argument name="Product Type" default="PRODUCT TYPE"}] (e.g., leather/fabric for clothing, metal/carbon for cars, organic ingredients for food). Include visible raw edges and stitching.

- Fasteners: Use strips of semi-transparent masking tape and black duct tape with the "[{argument name="Brand Name" default="BRAND NAME"}]" logo printed in white to "hold" the pieces together.

3. GRAPHIC OVERLAYS & STAMPS

- Stamp: A black, distressed ink stamp or seal near the hero product with text: "AUTHENTIC QUALITY // SUSTAINABLE CHOICE".

- Typography: Small, clean sans-serif and typewriter-style text blocks describing the heritage of [{argument name="Brand Name" default="BRAND NAME"}] placed at the bottom.

- Logo: The primary "[{argument name="Brand Name" default="BRAND NAME"}]" logo centered at the very bottom in a minimalist font.

4. STYLE & ATMOSPHERE

- Aesthetic: Industrial DIY, workshop moodboard, high-end editorial collage.

- Lighting: Bright, studio high-key lighting with soft, multi-layered shadows between the paper scraps and textures.

- Quality: 8K resolution, macro detail on paper grain, fabric fibers, and tape adhesive.

Sky: pale winter clouds with a break of warm sunrise light from the right, golden rays cutting through cold air, long blue shadows on snow, visible breath in the air. ## Photorealistic Portrait with Ukiyo-e and Industrial Fusion

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Model the image after the attached image of a woman. A profile portrait. Left-facing profile with a cool, intelligent expression. Bust-up composition.

The woman's face and skin must maintain completely photorealistic photographic quality. This is the highest priority, ensuring no illustration or anime style. Ultra-high-definition texture where every pore, fine hair, eyelash, and the translucency of the ear cartilage is visible.

The background and decorative elements are a fusion of urban, industrial, and Ukiyo-e (Japanese woodblock print). Grid lines and measurement lines run across the entire screen, but parts of the grid transform into the curves of Hokusai's waves. Straight design lines suddenly change into the swell of wave crests at a certain point. Numerical and scale notations are mixed with vertical Japanese text.

The sharp geometric triangles and lines placed in the background are indigo blue, the same deep blue as Ukiyo-e aizuri (indigo printing). Woodblock print wave patterns and swirling motifs are visible through the interior of the triangles. Geometry and Ukiyo-e patterns coexist in a single frame.

Miniature-sized people are working on the woman's face and contours. They are Edo-period Ukiyo-e carvers and printers, carving the woodblock with small chisels and printing on washi paper with baren. The Ukiyo-e artisans are wearing indigo kimonos. Small scaffolding and ladders are erected along the contours of the face.

The outline of the woman's profile is, in some parts, a sharp digital vector line, and in other parts, an Ukiyo-e outline drawn with ink and brush. Two line drawing techniques intersect on one person's face.

Ink splatters and droplets are scattered across the entire screen, with indigo ink splatters and vermilion splatters mixed in with black ink splatters. The grunge texture is a combination of concrete wall texture and washi paper fiber texture.

Absolutely no characters, text, typography, Japanese strings, seals, stamps, hanko, or signatures are included in the image.

Lighting features dramatic shadows with strong contrast. Hard light from the left side of the screen makes the profile contour stand out clearly with distinct light and shadow. Film grain. Ultra-high resolution. Sharp. Art poster style.
```

Surround the portrait with dynamic colorful paint splashes and expressive ink strokes, blending organically into the composition. Use bold yet controlled blue and orange tones for contrast and energy.
Background: layered vintage newspaper collage texture with subtle typography fragments, distressed paper details, and abstract paint splashes integrated naturally into the layout. The collage should feel artistic but clean, not cluttered.
Lighting: cinematic high-contrast lighting with dramatic highlights and controlled shadows, enhancing depth while maintaining natural facial realism.
Color grading: vibrant but balanced, strong blue and orange contrast, modern poster aesthetic.
Output: ultra-detailed, 4K resolution, clean edges, sharp rendering, professional poster art quality, modern digital illustration with realistic depth and premium finish
```

Shot on a Hasselblad 907X 50C with an XCD 90mm f/2.5 lens, aperture f/2.8 shallow depth of field emphasizes the precision fit of the suit while the background dissolves into painterly color patches; captured in 16-bit RAW with high dynamic range and soft film grain to preserve wool texture and the glow of colored light without "harsh" digital sharpness. Frame a tight portrait cropped to mid-chest, at eye level, with a slight three-quarter turn; the model is offset from center so the stained-glass pattern reads in the negative space. Expression restrained and thoughtful; the gaze passes just past the lens, as if distant music is playing. Incorporate a mix of the following textures selectively across different cells: Halftone dots (comic-book style raster patterns) 
Fabric or embroidery textures suggesting jersey material or stitched patches Film grain or heavy noise on high-contrast action shots The result should feel handcrafted and layered rather than digitally assembled. 

Color Strategy Base palette: monochrome black and white. Introduce selective color overlays only on specific grid cells. The accent colors should relate to the relevant team, brand, or national flag. Use color sparingly to create visual rhythm and emphasis.

## Retro Line Art Storefront Illustration Generator Template

> Minimal, retro logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[BRAND NAME] | [SUBJECT_TOGGLE]. Act as a Lead Graphic Designer.

PHASE 1: ARCHITECTURAL SETTING.

Create a minimalist 2D vector illustration of a vintage-style storefront representing [BRAND NAME]. 

- Autonomously design the facade (Modernist, Industrial, or Classic) to match the brand's heritage.

PHASE 2: SUBJECT LOGIC (TOGGLE SYSTEM).

- If [SUBJECT_TOGGLE] is "OFF": Show only the empty storefront.

- If [SUBJECT_TOGGLE] is "ON": Autonomously add 1-2 characters or a pet that fits the brand's lifestyle.

PHASE 3: DYNAMIC TYPOGRAPHY (CORRECTED).

- Header: Autonomously generate a high-status "Club" or "Community" title based on the nature of [BRAND NAME]. * Examples: For Starbucks — "Coffee Roasters Society"; for Nike — "Elite Runners Club"; for Apple — "Digital Creators Guild". 

- Branding: Place the [BRAND NAME] logo clearly on the storefront awning.

- Contextual Info: Below the awning, add "EST. [REAL FOUNDING YEAR]" and "[REAL ORIGIN CITY]".

PHASE 4: VISUAL STYLE.

- Two-tone palette: [BRAND SIGNATURE COLOR] on a pure white background.

- Style: 1950s-60s Retro Line Art. No gradients, no shadows.

TECH SPECS:

Flat 2D vector, 8k resolution, monochrome aesthetic, centered composition.
```

## Asimov Visionary Split-Scene Concept Art Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik arayüz tasarımı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Do this for Asimov:   1. The Analysis:  
      Analyze Input:   Identify the Visionary's Era (e.g., 1950s) and their Core Predictions (e.g., Robotics, AI).
      Left Side (The Origin):   Construct the room based on the  actual  historical workspace of the person (e.g., Typewriters, cigarette smoke, blueprints, tungsten light).
      Right Side (The Legacy):   Construct the room based on the  modern realization  of their ideas (e.g., Clean server room, holograms, advanced prosthetics, cool LED light).

  2. Composition (The Rift):  
      The Divide:   A diagonal beam of light splits the room.
      The Subject:   The Visionary stands dead center.
         Left Half of Body:  Dressed in period clothing, holding a pen/paper.
         Right Half of Body:  Fading into digital pixels or observing a floating hologram of their invention.
      The Wall:  
         Left Wall:  Covered in pinned sketches and paper notes.
         Right Wall:  A floor-to-ceiling window looking out onto a futuristic city (The world they predicted).

  3. Visual 
      Cohesion:   The perspective lines of the room must match perfectly across the split. A wooden desk on the left might morph into a sleek glass interface on the right.
      Lighting:   Warm/Dusty (Left) vs. Cold/Sterile (Right).

  Output:   ONE image, 16:9 Aspect Ratio, Cinematic Concept Art, Photorealistic.
```

 },

 },

 },

 },

 },

 ]
 },
 "negative_prompt": [

## Samurai Sniper Dynamic Battle Scene Prompt

> Sinematik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Masterpiece quality, theatrical anime-scale dynamic battle scene. A samurai character is the main subject (preserving a traditional samurai feel, optionally blended with subtle modern gear elements). Prone firing pose (low-profile/propped close to the roof surface), body flattened to the tiles, elbows braced for stability. Cheek pressed to the stock while peering through the scope, one eye narrowed, an intense focused stare and clenched teeth conveying tension and determination.

Composition and angle: the camera is placed almost on the roof tiles—an extreme ground-level ultra low-angle. Wide-angle lens with exaggerated perspective and strong depth. A diagonal composition: the sniper rifle runs from the extreme lower foreground into the midground, leading the viewer’s eye directly to the samurai’s gaze. The muzzle and muzzle flash dominate the extreme foreground, appearing oversized and close enough to “break the screen,” while the face (especially the eyes) stays crisp and sharp.

Action: captured at the exact instant of firing. A large, stylized muzzle flash erupts in the foreground; sparks, glowing embers, and thin smoke burst outward. Ejected brass and scattered casings on the tiles reinforce the sense of ongoing fire. A faint luminous streak suggests the bullet’s path slicing through the night air. Subtle recoil pushes the rifle and shoulder back; dynamic motion blur on the weapon and sleeves, while the eyes and brow remain tack-sharp. The scope glass reflects a cold blue-green sheen; metal surfaces show finely detailed reflections and crisp edge highlights.

Setting: the rooftop of a white Japanese castle keep (white plaster walls), under a clear night sky. Clean moonlight creates strong rim lighting along roof ridges and the samurai’s silhouette. Traditional roof features—curved ceramic tiles, ridge lines, gables, and shachihoko ornaments—read clearly in silhouette and detail. The firing shock and weight shift chip and crack tiles; fragments and dust jump up and arc toward the camera. High-contrast cinematic lighting with a dramatic warm–cool split: warm muzzle flash versus cool moonlight. Filmic particles (dust, embers) in the air.

Vertical cinematic poster composition, 9:16 aspect ratio, no text.
```

A massive yellow crane slowly lifts the top macaron into place while smaller cranes, excavators, and dump trucks operate below on a powdered-sugar terrain.

Ultra-detailed photorealistic textures contrast smooth dessert surfaces with worn construction machinery. Dreamlike but believable physics, gentle ambient motion, no abrupt cuts, seamless transitions. Vertical composition, epic scale contrast, surreal food-architecture world, cinematic depth of field, 8K realism, slow majestic pacing.

– cinematic realism
– slow motion accents

– surreal architectural fantasy

## Image-to-Image Titanic Movie Poster Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded image exactly as the base/reference. Do not change the subjects’ faces, poses, camera angle, or composition. Preserve facial features and expression. Recreate a vertical cinematic movie-poster based on this image with the following specifications:
Canvas: vertical poster, aspect ratio 2:3 (e.g. 4000×6000 px). High-res, photoreal, print-quality (minimum 300 DPI).
Composition: keep the ship’s bow centered and dominating the lower two-thirds; keep the couple faded and large in the top third as in the uploaded image (overlay/blend). Do not move or re-pose subjects.
Lighting & mood: cinematic golden-hour rim light on faces, soft warm highlights, cool deep shadows on the hull; strong contrast with gentle atmospheric mist; subtle film grain and slight vignette for vintage cinematic look.
Color grade: warm skin highlights, slightly desaturated midtones, deep navy/charcoal hull, soft cyan sky with white cloud details; natural skin texture preserved (no smoothing).
Ship details: maintain the bow’s perspective and railing lines; add realistic water spray/splash at the hull base, small white foam particles; keep deck rail details sharp.
Overlay blend: the couple at the top should be semi-translucent, softly feathered into the sky; maintain original hair, jewelry, and clothing details.
Title & typography: center the single word {argument name="title" default="TITANIC"} across the bottom third in large serif (Trajan Pro / classic film serif), all caps, wide tracking, pure white with subtle drop shadow and tiny bevel; place title exactly like the reference.
Effects/finishing: increase perceived sharpness on ship hull and title; keep couple slightly softer; apply subtle film grain, mild chromatic aberration, and filmic contrast curve.
Negative constraints: no additional people or logos, no modern artifacts (phones, text), no watermark, no unnatural skin smoothness, no heavy plastic look.
For img2img parameters (suggested): strength 0.35–0.55, preserve composition, seed optional for reproducibility. Output: 4000×6000 PNG, lossless.
Instruction to renderer: Use the uploaded photo as the primary source and only refine/augment per the above — do not replace or re-render faces or change the original pose/composition.
```

```
image_generation_prompt:
  meta:
    description: "Anime movie poster style illustration of a chibi character from behind, about to ride a road bike"
    reference_usage: "Character appearance refers to the attached image"
  
  style:
    art_style: "Anime movie poster"
    mood: "Dramatic"

  subject:
    type: "Chibi character"
    pose:
      view: "Back view"
      action: "Stepping forward with the left foot towards the road bike in the background (directly above)"
      target_direction: "Towards the road bike (directly above the image)"

  composition:
    top:
      element: "Red Road bike"
      layout: "Full width of the screen, parallel to the top edge of the image"
    center:
      element: "Back view of the chibi character"
      angle: "Overhead"
    bottom:
      text_layer_base:
        content: "{argument name="base text" default="KASANE"}"
        style: "bold sans-serif font"
        color: "White"
      text_layer_overlay:
        content: "{argument name="overlay text" default="カサネ"}"
        style: "Rough, brush-stroked characters"
        color: "Red"
        position: "Overlapping the KASANE text"

  objects:
    road_bike:
      color: "Red"
      orientation: "Side view / Horizontal"
      facing_direction: "Left"
    road:
      detail: "A straight white line is drawn in the center of the road. The white line is not clearly visible, only faintly and vaguely. A slight, faint crack is visible in a small part of the left side of the road."

  technical_settings:
    shot_size: "Long shot"
    shot_angle: "Overhead shot"
    zoom_emphasis: "Strong emphasis on the chibi character and the bicycle"
    lens_effects: "Vignetting"
    lighting:
      direction: "Illuminated from above"
```

Consistent character from reference image: exact same face, hairstyle, body shape, and original outfit. NO redesign.

[Composition & Pose]:
Heroic and exaggerated anime stance, waist-up framing, front-facing. Arms tightly crossed, chest pushed forward, leaning slightly towards the camera to create intense pressure. High visual density.

[Expression]:
Explosive emotion, intense anger mixed with determination. Mouth wide open shouting, eyebrows sharply angled, piercing eyes with sharp highlights.

[Dynamic Motion]:
A violent upward wind blasting from below the character,
powerful vertical airflow pushing both fabric and hair upward and outward.
The original clothing from the reference image remains intact and reacts naturally to the force,
all loose parts of the outfit whipping and flaring aggressively,
the character’s hair is lifted, blown upward, and spread by the same updraft,
hair strands follow the vertical flow with dynamic, forceful motion,
movement extends beyond the frame,
the motion feels heavy, chaotic, and explosive,
as if the character is standing in a violent upward draft.

[Art Style]:
Bold anime illustration, thick and rough energetic linework, expressive hand-drawn strokes. High contrast, dramatic cel-shading. Focus on raw emotion and impact.

[Background & Text]:
Solid white background.
Behind the character, ONE single instance of large handwritten Japanese brush text.
The text must appear ONCE only, not repeated, not duplicated.
All characters must be fully visible, complete, and readable.
No missing strokes, no cropped characters, no broken parts.

The text is continuous and written as one complete phrase:
“{argument name="dialogue" default="Who the hell do you think I am!"}”

Pure hand-painted brush lettering, NOT a font.
Wild, uneven brush strokes with controlled ink splatter and wobble,
but the structure of each character remains intact. Black ink only.

[Technical]:
High-impact poster visual, anime key visual style, ultra-high resolution, 8k.
```

## 3x3 Visual Storyboard for Product Branding

> Minimal, lüks logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A clean 3x3 [{argument name="aspect ratio" default="ratio"}] storyboard grid, composed of nine equally sized [{argument name="aspect ratio" default="ratio"}] frames, with an overall aspect ratio of [4:5].
Use the reference image as the basic reference for the product. The same product must be maintained in all nine frames, with completely consistent packaging design, brand logo, material, color, proportion, and overall visual identity. The product must be clearly discernible in every frame, and the label, logo, and proportion must remain exactly the same.
This storyboard is intended for brand visual display in high-end designer portfolios, focusing on form, composition, material expression, and visual rhythm, rather than realism or lifestyle narrative. The overall style should present a carefully planned, editorial, and strong design-oriented high-level aesthetic.
Frame 1: Front main visual product image, clean studio environment, neutral background, balanced composition, the product presents a calm, confident, and stable temperament.
Frame 2: Close-up shot, focus concentrated on the middle of the product, highlighting surface texture, material quality, and printing details.
Frame 3: The product is placed in an environment highly consistent with the brand and category, studio scene, inspired by the design elements and color scheme of the product itself.
Frame 4: The product in a state of use or interaction, with a neutral studio style background, restrained and simple hands and interaction elements, the overall style must be consistent with the packaging design.
Frame 5: Isometric perspective composition, presenting multiple products from an overhead isometric angle, arranged in precise geometric order, uniform angle, even spacing, clean image, clear structure, and strong graphic quality.
Frame 6: The product is slightly tilted and suspended in the air, with a neutral background consistent with the color scheme of the reference image. The suspension angle is natural and deliberately designed, presenting a stable and elegant floating state.
Frame 7: Extreme macro close-up, focusing on a specific detail of the label, edge, texture, or material.
Frame 8: The product is placed in an unexpected but extremely aesthetic and dynamic scene, with a bold style, editorial feel, and strong visual impact. The scene must be studio-shot and design-driven, with a dynamic composition that strengthens the brand tone.
Frame 9: Wide composition, showing the product in a usage context, placed in a refined, designed scene, with simple props and controlled styling, highly unified with the style of the entire set of images.
Camera and Style: Ultra-high quality studio photography, real camera texture. Different frames use different camera positions and compositions, precise depth of field control, strict lighting, and realistic materials and reflections. The overall light and shadow logic, color system, emotional atmosphere, and visual language must maintain high consistency across the nine frames, forming a complete and coherent series.
Output Requirement: Presented as a clean 3x3 grid, without borders, text, descriptions, or watermarks.
```

## Gritty Monochrome Music Poster Prompt with Neon Accents

> Retro, fütüristik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a bold, high-energy vertical music poster featuring a young female singer performing live on stage. She is captured mid-performance, holding a microphone close to her mouth, smiling with confidence and charisma. Her hair is styled in messy space buns with loose strands, and she wears an oversized black t-shirt layered with chunky chain necklaces, giving a rebellious, effortless stage presence. The portrait is treated in gritty monochrome with subtle grain and texture, while neon green accents are painted over parts of the image—especially flame-like graphic shapes rising from the bottom of her shirt—adding intensity and visual punch. The background is a flat, textured teal-green surface with visible noise and poster grain. Dominating the composition behind the subject is oversized, rounded, bold yellow typography spelling out "{argument name="text" default="ROCK STAR"}" in stacked lines. The letters are thick, playful, and slightly retro, filling most of the frame and partially obscured by the performer to create depth. Place her name behind the figure.
```

## Surreal Cinematic Digital Illustration Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform the uploaded image into a high-impact digital illustration using the image as a strict facial reference. Face Match (Strict): Face match must remain 100% exact — identical facial structure, proportions, jawline, cheekbones, nose shape, lips, beard density(if available in reference image) and trim, skin texture, hairline, hairstyle, and overall identity. Do not beautify, stylize, or alter facial features. Subject & Pose: A young Eastern European man is shown in a clean side-profile pose (not extreme profile), facing toward the right side of the frame. His head is slightly raised, chin gently lifted, conveying confidence, ambition, and forward vision. Expression is calm and self-assured. Clothing & Styling: The subject wears formal, modern clothing — a tailored dark blazer over a crisp shirt, minimal and elegant, reinforcing a professional and ambitious persona. No distracting accessories. Key Visual Element: He wears bold orange reflective sunglasses as the focal point. The lenses reflect a detailed modern cityscape with skyscrapers, urban roads, and green trees, symbolizing vision, ambition, and future goals. Color & Illustration Style: The face and clothing are rendered in muted grayscale tones, with realistic texture translated into a refined digital illustration style. Surrounding the subject are expressive abstract paint strokes and splashes in neon green and electric blue, adding energy and contrast without covering facial details. Background & Lighting: The background is dark and dramatic, layered with deep grey tones, subtle texture, and painterly motion. Cinematic lighting creates strong subject separation, clean highlights, and controlled shadows. Overall Aesthetic: Modern, cinematic, surreal digital illustration with a 2/3 photoreal-illustration balance and 1/3 abstract expression, poster-quality, high contrast, premium look. Aspect Ratio: 4:5
```

## Epic Chinese Fantasy Zodiac Deities Prompt (12 Characters)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Real human body + spiritual light phantom behind

Overall Style and Quality
Visual Style: Top-tier realistic cultivation scroll, surreal cinematic commercial blockbuster, hardcore Eastern fantasy aesthetic.
Core Parameters: 8K ultra-high resolution, film texture, real skin texture (rejecting plastic/anime feel).
Physical Rules: Realistic light and shadow simulation, emphasizing the character's gravity and physical feedback in a specific environment.
Composition Strategy: Ultra-wide horizontal composition. The twelve Zodiac incarnations are arranged scattered from left to right in the same grand immortal realm, with no visual blind spots.
Unified Worldview: The scene is set in the unified “Hongmeng Immortal Realm.” The environment gradually transitions from the chaotic spiritual veins on the left to the sea of clouds and immortal traces on the right. All characters share the same set of atmospheric light and shadow; the actual Zodiac animals are strictly prohibited from appearing.

Core Visual Features
• Character Form: All figures are real human forms with extremely high aesthetic value, retaining human facial features and skin texture.
• Zodiac Representation: Zodiac characteristics are embodied through the characters' clothing tailoring, color details, personal magical treasures, and the grand “Beast Spirit/Martial Soul” phantom composed of pure spiritual light behind the character.
• Beast Spirit Manifestation: The Beast Spirit appears as a translucent light state or phantom, entirely woven from flowing spiritual energy and radiance, lacking physical texture, enveloping the character from behind and above like a miracle, full of divinity.

Photography and Lighting
• Equipment: Canon EOS R5, 35mm prime lens.
• Lighting Design: Uniformly illuminated by the “Immortal Realm Morning Glow,” light enters from one side of the frame, creating the Tyndall effect.
• Ambient Light Compensation: The Beast Spirit light and shadow behind the character emit a soft innate spiritual light, refracting with the clouds, mist, and sword energy in the immortal realm.

Character Distribution and Detailed Description
1. Rat (Water): Far right, sea of clouds. Human form is a nimble youth in dark attire, with a massive, light-translucent Rat phantom suspended behind, eyes alert.
2. Ox (Earth): A steady, strong man in dark bronze heavy armor. Behind him stands a mountain-like Azure Ox light phantom, demonstrating the power to lift mountains.
3. Tiger (Wood): A dominant commander in a black and gold tiger-striped robe. Behind him is a Tiger Spirit light phantom, with flowing light and shadow, seemingly descending from the mountain.
4. Rabbit (Wood): A cool, ethereal fairy in a moon-white flowing immortal dress. Accompanied by a Jade Rabbit phantom, entirely condensed from white light and emitting soft radiance, creating an ethereal atmosphere.
5. Dragon (Metal): Occupying a high position, in a dark dragon scale robe, supremely noble. Behind him coils a hundred-zhang long, majestic and sacred Giant Dragon spiritual light phantom.
6. Snake (Fire): A seductive mystic in a purple snake-patterned satin robe. Behind her slithers a long, deep, Spiritual Snake phantom composed of purple flowing light.
7. Horse (Fire): A valiant sword cultivator in crimson battle gear. Behind him is a fiery Steed light phantom, hooves treading fire, ready to gallop.
8. Sheep (Earth): A gentle healer in a beige cotton and linen immortal dress. Behind her appears a peaceful, hazy White Sheep light phantom, like auspicious clouds.
9. Monkey (Metal): An untamed battle expert in battle-damaged gold armor. Behind him is a Monkey Spirit light phantom, holding a divine weapon and looking down upon the world.
10. Rooster (Metal): The dawn watcher, in a feather-cut battle robe. Wings spread behind him, revealing a flowing gold, radiant Phoenix light phantom, like a mirage.
11. Dog (Earth): A loyal general in leather and metal light armor. Behind him is an upright, protective Spiritual Dog light phantom.
12. Pig (Water): Far left, by the spiritual spring. A leisurely immortal in loose blue robes. Behind him swirls a deeply blessed Spiritual Pig phantom, composed of rich aura.

Final Vision
This is no longer a collection of isolated individuals, but an epic assembly of the twelve Zodiac deities in a unified immortal realm. Through the visual structure of “Real Human Body + Spiritual Light Phantom Behind,” it perfectly balances the emotional beauty of humans with the mythological depth of the Zodiac, constructing the ultimate Eastern fantasy universe.
```

## Sanrio Tarot Card Risograph Simulation

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a single Major Arcana tarot card featuring one randomly selected official Sanrio character from this list: Hello Kitty, My Melody, Kuromi, Cinnamoroll, Pompompurin, Keroppi, Badtz-Maru, Little Twin Stars (Kiki & Lala), Chococat, Tuxedo Sam, Gudetama, Aggretsuko, Hangyodon, Dear Daniel, or Usahana. CRITICAL: The character MUST be assigned a randomly chosen zodiac theme AND a randomly chosen Major Arcana card (The Fool, The Magician, The High Priestess, The Empress, The Emperor, The Hierophant, The Lovers, The Chariot, Strength, The Hermit, Wheel of Fortune, Justice, The Hanged Man, Death, Temperance, The Devil, The Tower, The Star, The Moon, The Sun, Judgement, The World) - both assignments are non-negotiable and mandatory. The zodiac symbolism must be woven into the tarot card's meaning and visual metaphors. Render in traditional tarot card proportions (2.75:4.75 ratio, portrait orientation) with an ornate decorative border featuring Art Nouveau-inspired flowing lines, celestial motifs, and geometric patterns that reference both the zodiac sign and tarot symbolism. The character must be depicted with 100% canonical accuracy maintaining exact official Sanrio proportions and features, positioned centrally as the card's main subject in a pose that embodies the tarot card's archetypal meaning. The entire composition must be a high-quality risograph print simulation featuring authentic soy-based ink grain texture that mimics absorption on uncoated 80-120gsm paper stock, stochastic dot stippling using randomized diffusion dither patterns (not regular halftone grids), fine sand-like noise within all color fills simulating Grain Touch screening mode, organic ink density variations creating tonal shifts without smooth gradients, slight dot gain effect at color boundaries, and spot color separation logic with translucent ink layering behavior rather than CMYK process colors. Colors must be bold, flat, vibrant, and fully saturated (80-100% chroma) with maximum brightness, utilizing fluorescent ink vibrancy where appropriate and showing no fading, desaturation, or washed-out pastels. The border design should use a maximum of 3-4 spot colors plus black, with the character surrounded by a thick uniform black outline (3-4pt stroke weight). Background within the border should be cream/off-white (#FFF8E7) to simulate aged tarot card stock. The output must be 3000×5200px resolution in sRGB, containing absolutely no text labels, character names, tarot card titles, zodiac symbols (♈♉♊♋♌♍♎♏♐♑♒♓), Roman numerals, descriptive typography, or dimensional effects like drop shadows.
```

## 2000s Scrapbook Collage Illustration

```
A vibrant 2000s scrapbook-style collage featuring the same young man in multiple cutout poses. One portrait is centered and large, showing him wearing round glasses and a black hoodie, making a finger-heart gesture toward the camera. Other cutouts show him squatting with a vintage instant camera, and another blowing a pink bubblegum bubble.  

The background looks like an open sketchbook with textured paper, filled with colorful paint strokes in rainbow tones, playful doodles, arrows, stars, hearts, butterflies, smiley faces, and pop-art stickers. Bold, bubbly typography stickers read phrases like “2000s Vibes,” “Dream Big,” “Be Yourself,” “Cool Dude,” “Say Cheese,” and “Pop!”  

The overall aesthetic is nostalgic, cheerful, and youthful — inspired by early 2000s teen magazines and scrapbook journals. High saturation, soft lighting, clean white sticker outlines around each cutout, whimsical and fun mood, creative flat-lay composition, digital collage illustration style.
```

## Arnold Schwarzenegger Silhouette with Volumetric Mountain Forest

> Fotogerçekçi manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
**Prompt Title:** The Austrian Oak's Volumetric Anatomy

**Prompt:**

A monumental, highly graphic, high-contrast silhouette of Arnold Schwarzenegger in his 1970s prime, perfectly executing a classic Side Chest pose. The exterior outline of the physique is rendered as a razor-sharp, pure black (0% light) shape, creating an extreme visual boundary.

The entire internal negative space within the silhouette is intricately filled with a photorealistic, dense Austrian mountain forest landscape—specifically, deep coniferous pines and firs of the Hohe Tauern region. The scene within the body is captured at the peak of Golden Hour sunset. Deep, rich hues of fire orange, crimson, and violet dominate the internal sky.

Crucially, intense, focused volumetric god rays and sun shafts cut dramatically through the thick forest canopy, filtering dust and mist. These light shafts meticulously trace and illuminate the underlying anatomy: the beams define the striations of the pectoral muscle, the peaks of the biceps, and the separation of the deltoids. The light filtering through the trees *is* the muscle definition.

**Camera Settings:**
Shot on Hasselblad X2D, 135mm prime lens. Wide aperture (f/2.8) for maximum environmental detail within the silhouette.

**Lighting Details:**
Extreme hard backlighting defines the silhouette against a neutral, slightly hazy background. Internal lighting is Deep Golden Hour, intense volumetric mist/fog, strong rim light defining individual tree branches and forming anatomical detail.

**Stylistic Notes:**
Masterpiece cinematic photography. Hyper-detailed realism within the silhouette. Maximal resolution. Aspect ratio 16:9. --ar 16:9 --v 6.0 --s 750
```

## Music Curation: Audio-Visual Dioramas for Pink Floyd Fans

> Fotogerçekçi, fütüristik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Do this for {argument name="input band" default="Pink Floyd"}: Input Variable: [INSERT BAND/ALBUM] (e.g., Pink Floyd, Daft Punk, Nirvana)
System Instruction:
Act as a Music Historian and Set Designer. Create a 2x2 Grid of "Audio-Visual Dioramas."
1. The Curation Logic (The Deep Cuts):
Analyze Input: Identify the genre and sonic signature of the Input Band.
Select 4 Hidden Gems: Identify 4 lesser-known albums from other bands that share this sonic DNA but offer a different visual flavor.
The Translation: For each album, define the "Sonic Materiality":
If Heavy Metal: Rusty Iron, Fire, Spikes.
If Synth-Pop: Neon Glass, Chrome, Laser Grids.
If Folk/Acoustic: Mossy Wood, Stone, Candlelight.
If Prog Rock: Surreal Marble, Floating Prisms, Clouds.
2. The "Immutable" Container (The Vinyl Stage):
The Base: Each diorama is built on top of a Spinning Vinyl Record acting as the circular stage floor.
The Plinth: The record sits on a high-end, thick Audiophile Turntable Base (Wood or Brushed Aluminum).
The Nameplate (CRITICAL TEXT FIX): On the vertical front face of the Plinth, there is a Polished Brass or Digital Display Plate.
Text Rule: It must clearly display the [ARTIST NAME] in bold, legible, capital letters.
3. The Composition (The Sound Made Solid):
CRITICAL: Do not just recreate the album cover. Build a world that feels like the music.
The Structure: Architecture rises from the grooves of the vinyl record.
Example: A wall of amplifiers, a surreal floating prism, a crumbling brick wall, or a forest of microphone stands.
The Atmosphere: Use particle effects to visualize the sound waves.
Visuals: Floating musical notes made of smoke, vibrating liquid surfaces, or shattered glass suspended in mid-air.
The Figures: Tiny 1:87 scale band members performing inside this surreal landscape.
4. Visual Syntax (Per Panel):
Panel A: Album 1 (Focus on Surrealism/Scale).
Panel B: Album 2 (Focus on Technology/Machinery).
Panel C: Album 3 (Focus on Nature/Organic Textures).
Panel D: Album 4 (Focus on Light/Color).
5. Lighting & Atmosphere:
Concert Lighting: Each box is lit like a stage show. Spotlights, laser beams, and stage haze.
Depth: Shallow depth of field (Tilt-Shift) to make the vinyl record look massive.
Background: Seamless Slate Grey to keep the focus on the colors.
Output: 2x2 Grid, Hyper-Realistic Miniature Set Design, 8k Resolution, "Audiophile" Aesthetic.
```

## The Rock 'Lay the Smackdown' Cinematic Visual

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A colossal, photorealistic studio shot centered on the phrase **"{argument name="phrase" default="LAY THE SMACKDOWN"}**" meticulously constructed as a three-dimensional sculpture. The letters are built entirely from precision-machined, miniature fitness equipment: polished chrome dumbbells, textured black cast-iron kettlebells, and deeply etched micro-weight plates. The sculpture exhibits extreme detail, showing fingerprints on the polished metal and subtle chalk dust on the iron components.

Standing just behind the sculpture is Dwayne "The Rock" Johnson, captured mid-pose, radiating immense confidence. He is wearing a tailored, deep black suit jacket over a high-neck fitted t-shirt, emphasizing his physique. His gaze is intense, directed slightly off-camera.

**Visual Style:** High-contrast cinematic studio photography, resembling a blockbuster movie title card. Deep saturation, razor sharpness, and immense texture across all surfaces.

**Setting and Lighting:** Set against a monolithic, seamless matte black backdrop. The scene is illuminated by dramatic, controlled studio lighting. Hard, focused key light highlights the chrome surfaces with brilliant specular reflection. A powerful amber-orange rim light defines Johnson's outline and separates the sculpture from the darkness, creating a sense of heat and power. Subtle atmospheric haze enhances the volumetric lighting effects.

**Camera Settings:** Shot on Hasselblad X2D, 85mm prime lens. Aperture f/2.0. Extremely shallow depth of field (DoF) isolating the foreground text and the subject. Cinematic color grade, ultra-detailed texture mapping.

**Aspect Ratio:** --ar 16:9 --style raw --v 6.0
```

## Architectural Typography Micro-World Render Prompt (New York)

> Fotogerçekçi, fütüristik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Do this for {argument name="city" default="New York"}: System Instruction:
Generate a hyper-realistic 3D architectural typography render. The letters must act as massive, hollow containers housing a "Micro-World" that semantically matches the Input Word.
1. Semantic Inference (The Blueprint):
Analyze the Input Word to determine the Biome, Material, and Lighting:
If Organic (e.g., JUNGLE, LIFE): Material = Glass & Wood. Interior = Vines, Waterfalls, Trees. Light = Sunlight/Green.
If Urban (e.g., CITY, NYC): Material = Steel & Concrete. Interior = Skyscrapers, Traffic, Streetlights. Light = Amber/Blue.
If Industrial (e.g., FACTORY, RUST): Material = Rusted Iron & Mesh. Interior = Pipes, Steam, Gears. Light = Orange Furnace glow.
If Abstract (e.g., LOVE, HOPE): Material = Gold or White Marble. Interior = Floating petals, soft clouds, candles. Light = Soft Pink/Gold.
2. The Immutable Container (The Mega-Letters):
Geometry: Massive, blocky sans-serif typography.
The Shell: The letterforms are Hollow Containers with open faces or glass walls. They act like terrariums or shadow boxes.
The Base: The letters rest on a surface relevant to the theme (e.g., A map for Cities; Moss for Nature; Blueprint for Industry).
3. The Internal Ecosystem (The Diorama):
CRITICAL: The "World" lives inside the negative space of the letters.
The Density: Fill the interior volume with thousands of tiny details (Micro-Scale).
Example: If "TOKYO," tiny neon signs, vending machines, and pedestrians fill the inside of the "T".
The Verticality: Use the height of the letters. Elevators, vines, or ladders should climb up the vertical strokes.
4. Visual Syntax:
Scale: Tilt-Shift Focus. Blur the background to make the letters look like tangible miniature models.
Detail: High-frequency textures. We need to see individual leaves, bricks, or rivets.
5. Lighting:
Internal Glow: The letters are lit from within by the bioluminescence or streetlights of the micro-world.
External Sun: A soft studio key light highlights the rim of the letter casing.
Output: 3D Render, Octane/Redshift, "Micro-World" Aesthetic, 8k Resolution.
```

## High-End Sports Graphic Poster Template

> Lüks logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[PERSON NAME].
Act as a high-end sports graphic designer creating a conceptual tribute poster. The style is a complex "dual exposure photo-grid composite" with mixed-media textures.
CENTRAL STRUCTURE (THE VESSEL):
The central focus is a large-scale, high-contrast black and white portrait silhouette of {argument name="person name" default="[PERSON NAME]"}. This main portrait acts as the container.
THE GRID FILL & TEXTURES (MIXED MEDIA):
The interior of the silhouette is populated by a dense "photo mosaic grid" of action shots from the person's career.
CRITICAL TEXTURE INSTRUCTION: Do not just paste flat photos. Apply artistic textures to various grid cells to create a tactile, collage feel. Use effects like:
Halftone Dots: Comic-book style raster patterns on some cells.
Fabric/Embroidery: Subtle thread or canvas textures suggesting a jersey or patch.
Film Grain: Heavy noise on specific high-contrast action shots.
COLOR STRATEGY:
The base is Monochrome B&W. Use selective color overlays (relevant to the team/flag) ONLY on specific grid cells to create a rhythm.
TYPOGRAPHY & BRANDING (STRICT MICRO-SCALING):
Top Left (The Name): Write "{argument name="person name 2" default="[PERSON NAME]"}" strictly using the font Inter Semibold.
Kerning: Tight negative kerning (-4%).
Size: SMALL and discreet. It must occupy MAXIMUM 20% of the canvas width. Do NOT make it large or loud.
Top Right (The Symbol): Place the primary logo (Team/Brand/Flag).
Size: VERY SMALL. It must occupy MAXIMUM 10% of the canvas width.
COMPOSITION & BACKGROUND:
Background: Off-white or light grey with a visible high-quality paper or concrete texture. It should not be flat digital white.
Alignment: Center the figure perfectly. Maintain wide negative space around the object.
```

## Parametrized Movie Poster Generation Prompt

> Sinematik, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
DIRECTIVE:
Generate a high-end, vertical format Movie Poster. Act as an expert Art Director and execute the following prompt based strictly on the defined INPUT VARIABLES and any ATTACHED IMAGES.

INPUT VARIABLES (FILL THESE OUT):
{VAR_TITLE}: [{argument name="movie title" default="INSERT MOVIE TITLE HERE"}]

{VAR_CREATOR}: [{argument name="creator name" default="INSERT CREATOR/DIRECTOR NAME HERE"}]

{VAR_STYLE}: [{argument name="genre or art style" default="INSERT GENRE OR ART STYLE HERE"}]

{VAR_PALETTE}: [{argument name="color palette" default="INSERT COLOR PALETTE HERE"}]

EXECUTION PLAN:

SUBJECT & REFERENCE HANDLING (CONDITIONAL LOGIC):

IF IMAGES ARE ATTACHED: You must use the attached images as the primary visual reference. The protagonist on the poster must STRICTLY maintain the facial features, likeness, and physical attributes of the reference images.

IF NO IMAGES ARE ATTACHED: You have full creative freedom to design an original protagonist that perfectly fits the {VAR_STYLE} and the {VAR_CREATOR} aesthetic.

ARTISTIC DIRECTION:

Visual Style: Render the entire image in the specific style of {VAR_STYLE}.

Signature Look: Apply the directorial techniques associated with {VAR_CREATOR} (like signature camera angles, lighting choices, or visual composition habits).

Color Grading: The whole vibe, lighting, and shadows should be fully driven by the {VAR_PALETTE}. Go for high contrast and make sure the mood matches the color palette.

TYPOGRAPHY & LAYOUT:

Title: Display the text "{VAR_TITLE}" front and center (top or bottom placement).

Font: Pick a typeface that fits both the {VAR_STYLE} and the feel of {VAR_CREATOR}.

Poster Elements: Add a subtle billing block (credits) at the bottom to keep it realistic.

TECHNICAL SPECS:

Aspect Ratio: Vertical (9:16 or 2:3)

Resolution: 8K, ultra-detailed

Finish: Commercial print quality, sharp subject focus, cinematic depth of field
```

Reference Image 1 Look × Reference Image 2 High-End Gentleman Aesthetic

[Core Directive]
As a world-class fashion photographer (Steven Meisel/Paolo Roversi/Peter Lindbergh), create a high-end fashion portrait suitable for the cover style of 'British Vogue' for **[the person in Reference Image 1]**.
Four Core Requirements:

1. Strictly reproduce 100% of the external features of the person in Reference Image 1 (hair style, facial contours, features, age perception, body type, etc.) (without any pre-settings or assumptions).

3. Fully incorporate the visual DNA of Reference Figure 2 (seated posture with hand supporting the cheek, fine pinstripe suit, folding chair, pure white background, monochrome tone, relaxed and sophisticated atmosphere).
4. Allow creative fine-tuning (flexible adjustments permitted while maintaining the core aesthetic).

[I. Subject Appearance Reproduction - Reference Figure 1]

Features that must be completely retained:

Hair style: Hair length, color, style, curl degree, hairline, volume, etc. - Complete match
Face shape: Contour, width, jawline, facial proportions - Complete retention
Five senses: Shape, size, position, and proportion of eyes, nose, lips, ears - Complete match
Skin: Skin color, texture, moles, scars, blemishes, etc. - Complete retention
Age characteristics: Wrinkles, fine lines, under-eye bags, nasolabial folds, etc. - Realistically reproduced
Body type: Proportion, shoulder width, overall physique - Complete retention
Unique characteristics: Individual specific facial or physical characteristics

❌ Absolute Prohibitions:

No external feature of Reference Image 1 shall be altered
Reference Image 1 features shall not be 'optimized' or 'beautified'
No pre-setting or assumption of how Reference Image 1 should look
Reference Image 1's appearance shall not be changed due to the style of Reference Image 2

✅ Verification Criteria:

Viewers who know the person in Reference Figure 1 must instantly recognize them as the same person
Facial feature similarity must be 95% or higher
Failure if pointed out that the image does not resemble Reference Figure 1

[II. Expression and Atmosphere - Dignified and Relaxed Gentleman]
Direction of Atmosphere Transformation:
Core Emotional Keywords:

'Relaxed confidence'
'Sophistication in idleness'
'Subtle high-end feel'
'Artistic distance'
'The composure of a fashionable gentleman'

Facial Expression (Reference Figure 2 style):
Gaze:

✅ Slightly raise the gaze and look straight at the camera

A gentle yet sharp gaze
Conveying 'I am relaxed, but aware of my charm'
An observant eye with artistic distance
Not exaggerated, effortless, and not flattering

Mouth:

✅ Lightly tighten the corners of the mouth or close naturally

No smiling, not serious
A completely relaxed, natural state
Conveying 'subtle sophistication'
Slightly pursing the lips is also an option (restrained elegance)

Overall Facial Impression:

✅ Completely relax the facial muscles

No tension or deliberate movement whatsoever
Natural, relaxed, and composed state
Conveying the message 'I don't need to prove anything'

✅ Naturally express skin texture

Realistic texture while applying moderate correction
High-end and refined, but authentic texture

Emotional Guidance by Director:

'You are the person on the cover of Vogue. You are very relaxed.'
'You are not posing; you are just sitting here.'
'Your languor is real, not an act.'
'Fashion is about relaxing, not striving.'

[III. Core Pose - Seated Posture with Hand Supporting Cheek (Reference Figure 2 Standard Pose)]
Iconic Pose:
Sitting on a folding chair:

✅ Sitting Style:

Sitting on a simple metal folding chair
Body slightly leaning forward or naturally upright
Legs naturally spread (shoulder-width or slightly wider)
Both feet placed flat on the ground

Hand Posture (Core Feature):

✅ Supporting the cheek with one hand (Refer to Figure 2 standard):

Place the elbow of the right or left hand on the thigh of the same side
Support the chin/cheek with the palm or fingers
An impression of sophistication despite appearing casual
Expressing a state of 'contemplation' or 'leisurely waiting'

✅ The other hand:

Naturally placed on the backrest/armrest of the chair
Or naturally hanging down
Or lightly placed on the opposite thigh

Significance of Posture:

Breaking the formality of stiffness
Creating a fashionable look of 'relaxed high-end feel'
Conveying the message 'Thinking about art/life'
Everyday object (folding chair) + High-end styling = Contrast aesthetic

Body Posture:

Relax the shoulders (do not raise them)
Naturally curve the spine (do not stiffen)
Overall conveying comfort, naturalness, and composure

Head Posture:

Head slightly turned sideways (5-15 degrees)
Or facing the camera straight on
Naturally tilted according to the position of the hand supporting the cheek

[IV. Styling Design - High-End Fashion Formal]
Attire (Refined Formal + Relaxed Details):
Core Item - Fine Pinstripe Suit (Reference Figure 2 Standard):

✅ Jacket:

Fine pinstripe in a {argument name="suit color" default="dark color"} (black/dark gray/navy)
Slim fit or standard silhouette
Double-breasted or single-breasted
Lapels are sharp, shoulder line is crisp

✅ Trousers:

Fine pinstripe of the same pattern as the jacket
High waist, slim fit or straight
Cuffed hem (Refer to Figure 2 important detail)

Cuff the hem 1-2 times
Exposing the ankles and socks
Softening the formality and adding a relaxed fashion feel

✅ Shirt:

White formal shirt
Collar neatly arranged, buttons fastened to the top or slightly loosened
Shirt cuffs visible 1-2 cm from the suit sleeves

Shoes and Socks:

✅ Black glossy leather shoes

Derby shoes or Oxford shoes
Highly polished, sharp reflection

✅ Black long socks (Refer to Figure 2 feature)

Clearly visible due to the cuffed hem
Solid color or fine stripe

Overall Style:

'High-end bespoke suit + relaxed fashionable details'
'Freedom within rigor'
'Refined but not stiff'

Accessories (Refined Details):
1. Thin-frame glasses (Recommended):

✅ Thin metal frame (gold/silver/black)
✅ Square or round frame
✅ Clear lenses
✅ Adds an 'intellectual fashion' atmosphere

2. Watch:

✅ Meta
```

{
  "prompt": "Use the uploaded reference image strictly for the original face and identity (do not alter facial features). Ultra-realistic materials, clean modern composition, futuristic athletic fashion aesthetic with a joyful, premium sportswear editorial mood. High-resolution, premium advertising quality, no logos, no clutter.",
  "style": "high-fashion sportswear editorial",
  "lighting": "bright soft studio lighting",
  "mood": "joyful, energetic, futuristic",
  "quality": "ultra-realistic, premium advertising",
  "resolution": "8K",

}

## Detailed Calendar Design Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Minimal, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
meta:
  title: "CALENDAR 2026 — {argument name="month name" default="FEBRUARY"} (Winter Alpine / Flat Teal Splitter / Sundays Red)"
  purpose: "image_generation_prompt"
  language_in_image: "English"
  output: "single calendar poster"
  quality: "commercial, crisp typography, no artifacts"
  strict_text_and_dates: true

canvas:
  size: { width: 2048, height: 1536 }
  aspect: "4:3 landscape"
  base_bg: "#FFFFFF"
  safe_margin_pct: 6

style:
  keywords:
    - "premium modern minimal"
    - "corporate clean"
    - "cool winter calm"
    - "high legibility"
  palette:
    deep_teal: "#083D57"
    teal: "#0E7890"
    mid_teal: "#6A90A0"
    navy: "#0B2433"
    text_dark: "#2C2F33"
    border_light: "#D7DDE2"
    icy: "#EAF2F6"
    saturday_blue: "#1F7FA0"
    sunday_red: "#C93B3B"
    holiday_red: "#C93B3B"
  typography:
    font: "Noto Sans (fallback: Helvetica/Arial)"
    rules:
      - "no warping, no jitter, no random glyphs"
      - "high anti-aliasing + optical kerning"

composition:
  layout:
    - "Left ~52%: winter alpine photo inside a large smooth curved mask"
    - "Right ~48%: clean white negative space with month + calendar card"
    - "Bottom-left: deep teal layered wave band hosting 'CALENDAR 2026'"
  photo_left:
    subject:
      - "snowy mountains, conifer forest, calm valley, overcast sky"
      - "no people, no buildings, no animals, no text"
    grading: "cool-neutral, slightly desaturated, moderate contrast, preserve snow detail"
    overlay: "very subtle snow/bokeh particles mainly over photo area (keep right area clean)"
  divider_splitter:
    shape: "thin smooth curved band near the boundary of photo/white"
    thickness: "5–7% of canvas height"
    fill_gradient: { from: "#083D57", to: "#0E7890" }
    optional_rim:
      enabled: true
      thickness: "1–2% of canvas height"
      fill_gradient: { from: "#EAF2F6", to: "#FFFFFF" }
    flat_rules:
      - "no 3D, no bevel, no inner shadow, no glass, no facets, no specular"
  bottom_waves:
    coverage: "bottom 22–28% of canvas"
    layers:
      - { gradient: { from: "#083D57", to: "#0B2433" }, opacity: 1.0 }
      - { gradient: { from: "#0E7890", to: "#6A90A0" }, opacity: 0.55 }
      - { gradient: { from: "#EAF2F6", to: "#FFFFFF" }, opacity: 0.45 }
    rule: "flat premium (no facets/bevel), minimal noise only to prevent banding"

text_blocks:
  month_number:
    text: "2"
    position: "top-right of right area"
    style: { weight: 800, color: "#0E7890", size: "very large" }
  month_label:
    text: "FEBRUARY"
    position: "upper-center of right area"
    style: { weight: 800, color: "#2C2F33", size: "large", tracking: "slightly wide" }
  footer_left:
    position: "bottom-left on teal wave band"
    lines:
      - { text: "CALENDAR", style: { weight: 700, color: "#FFFFFF", size: "medium" } }
      - { text: "2026",     style: { weight: 900, color: "#FFFFFF", size: "very large" } }
    alignment: "left"

calendar_card:
  placement: "center-right"
```

## Miniature World in a Liquid Stroke Template

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A {argument name="macro medium" default="[MACRO MEDIUM - e.g., stream of liquid gold, plume of smoke, silk ribbon]"}, captured mid-motion, forming a dynamic {argument name="shape" default="[SHAPE - e.g., arc, spiral, wave]"} from the lower left to the upper right. Suspended within the [TEXTURE/MATERIAL] lives a miniature world of [MICRO SUBJECT - e.g., a city, a historical event, an ecosystem], rendered as highly detailed structures sculpted entirely from the [MACRO MEDIUM] itself.

Inside the [MEDIUM]: [DETAIL 1], [DETAIL 2], and [DETAIL 3]. The forms appear [ADJECTIVE - e.g., woven, liquid, crystalline, glowing], strictly defined by the physics of the [MACRO MEDIUM].

Style: [PHOTOGRAPHY STYLE - e.g., Macro product photography] fused with {argument name="artistic genre" default="[ARTISTIC GENRE - e.g., Ukiyo-e, Cyberpunk, Baroque painting]"}. High emphasis on texture, lighting, and material contrast. Color palette: [COLORS] derived naturally from the material.

View & background:Plain, matte [BACKGROUND COLOR - usually Black or White] to create maximum contrast. Minimalist composition.

Typography:Title: “[TITLE]” Subtitle: “[SUBTITLE]” Font style: [FONT DESCRIPTION] placed cleanly in the negative space.

Composition rules:The [MEDIUM] creates a defined boundary. All details remain strictly inside the stroke/flow; nothing exists in the negative space. 8K, hyper-realistic texture, [LIGHTING STYLE].
```
