## New Year's Osechi Dish Advertisement Key Visual Generation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Character Name: [Set {argument name="character name" default="character name"} here]

Advertisement/Poster/Promotional Key Visual Generation Prompt
The primary purpose of this prompt is to generate a key visual for advertisements, posters, and promotional materials for high-end department stores, luxury restaurants, and limited sales, specifically featuring **"photorealistic images of Osechi dishes."**
The generated image must prioritize the following:

Completeness recognized instantly as an "advertisement."
The Osechi is the "main product," strongly boosted by the tiered structure, menu, world view, and character elements.

Overall Visual Premise (Advertising Declaration | Boost Design)
This key visual is based on an advertising design where the direction of expression changes significantly, starting from the characteristics of the reference image. This prompt does not aim for a stable solution but extracts the strongest element (luxury, strangeness, humor, specialty, etc.) from the reference image's intensity, boosting the entire advertising expression through high-quality, high-sense, and outrageously genius representation. The direction is automatically determined by the impression of the reference image. However, regardless of the direction, the image must be conscious of being an "advertisement that works in a department store's New Year's sales area." This boost result is consistently reflected and visualized in all subsequent composition, tiered structure, copy, and menu details.
Scene/Moment (The Instant Captured for the Advertisement)
Automatically select the moment that conveys the highest appeal as an advertisement and instantly communicates "what kind of existence" the Osechi is. This prompt does not fixate on a specific production pattern; the "instant captured" is defined as the state among the tiered box, food, structure, and world view that maximizes information density and narrative potential.
The tiered box may be closed or open.
The entire dish may be visible, or only a part may be emphasized.
Time is not fixed (just before, just after, or during movement).
It must be a high-quality, high-sense, and uniquely captivating Osechi that instantly grabs attention, and the optimal structure, angle, and state are automatically selected for this purpose.
Composition/Shooting Style (Advertising Layout Premise)
Place the Osechi tiered box in the center or slightly foreground of the screen.
High-end food advertisement angle, from the front to slightly diagonally above.
Intentional negative space for placing copy and menu details.
Technical Requirements:
8K resolution
Hyper-realistic food photography
Department store/luxury restaurant advertisement quality
Packaging/Tiered Box Design
The tiered box is designed as the **"outer shell" that most purely reflects the characteristics of the reference image.** The basic structure starts with a "tiered box suitable for supervision by a department store or long-established store," but it changes freely, becoming high-quality, high-sense, and bizarre, depending on the impression of the reference image.
Osechi Body (Product Persuasiveness)
The food composition is not limited to "Japanese" but is designed as a celebratory dish freely expanded based on the individuality of the reference image.
It may follow traditional Osechi or deviate from it; the food system most appropriate for the impression of the reference image is reconstructed each time. What is important is not the style, but that luxury, festivity, and specialness are established at a glance.
Character Expression (Edible Celebration World)
All characters are entities composed of food ingredients, exaggerating the characteristics of the reference image and reinterpreted into New Year's costumes matching the concept.
Although the materials are food, priority is given to expressions that make them feel as if they are breathing, moving, and vibrant in that world.
The characters are not treated as "creations" but as beings that have existed in the Osechi world from the beginning.
Color Design
Color rules are not fixed but automatically determined as the optimal solution derived from the reference image and concept.
The food, tiered box, characters, and background establish luxury and festivity through texture, density, and contrast, rather than color.
Advertising Copy/Menu Design (Most Important)
Copy and menu details naturally arise as a result of verbalizing the characteristics and concept of the reference image.
Text is placed on the screen as a "visual element intended to evoke a feeling instantly."
The menu details are a list of dish names that cleverly twist the characteristics of the reference image with high-sense and intense humor.
The {argument name="year" default="2026"} year display, selection concept, official seal, and main price are placed in effective positions using high-sense, high-quality, ultra-unique, and genius decorative text that matches the concept.
Variation Display (For Advertisement)
Display Single-tier/Two-tier/Three-tier in a wipe-style.
Small frame + item name + price.
Size that does not interfere with the main tiered box.
Background/Lighting
Based on soft New Year's light, but arranged according to the characteristics of the reference image.
Maximize the luster of lacquer, gold leaf, and food ingredients.
Final Tone (Output Guideline)
An advertising visual that seems genuinely for sale but transcends reality—where celebration and strangeness arise simultaneously.
The complete form of Character × Osechi × Advertising Key Visual.
```

## Chinese New Year Portrait with Red Knitwear and Minimalist Studio Look

> Minimal, retro moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
I. Theme and Emotion
1 Festive Theme: A fresh studio portrait celebrating the arrival of 2026, sweet, cute, relaxed, and playful.
2 Visual Temperament: Extreme minimalism (negative space), fashion editorial feel, clean, bright, gentle, and restrained.

II. Composition and Perspective
1 Aspect Ratio: Vertical, subject centered slightly lower, large area above reserved for text elements.
2 Composition Method: Primarily symmetrical and centered, the main subject is fully visible, feet and seat are visible, background negative space emphasizes the theme.
3 Depth of Field: Subject is sharp, background text is blurred to create layers and atmosphere.

III. Character and Pose
1 Character Setting: Young female, translucent and natural skin, light but refined makeup.
2 Expression and Action: Single eye winking, hands cupping the face in a flower-holding gesture, conveying playfulness and approachability.
3 Sitting Posture: Legs together and slightly tucked, body leaning forward slightly, overall line is compact and cute.

IV. Apparel and Styling
1 Main Clothing Color: {argument name="clothing color" default="Positive red"} thick cable-knit long dress or long sweater, covering to the calf, strong festive feel.
2 Accessory Details: Hair is half-up or symmetrically tied, paired with small dark hair accessories, overall simple.
3 Shoes and Socks: White mid-calf socks, black shiny round-toe shallow shoes, emphasizing vintage sweetness.

V. Scene and Props
1 Prop Selection: Transparent acrylic square stool, barely noticeable, enhancing minimalism and transparency.
2 Scene Type: Pure studio background without extra furnishings, emphasizing the subject and text theme.

VI. Background and Text Elements
1 Background Color: Warm light beige or light gray-beige, soft and non-glaring.
2 Text Design: Large handwritten cursive text running through the image. Text content: “{argument name="text content" default="2026 Hello!"}”
3 Text Function: Serves as a celebratory marker and visual rhythm line, enhancing layers and visual guidance.

VII. Lighting and Color
1 Lighting Style: Soft, uniform high-key studio lighting, very shallow shadows, smooth and three-dimensional facial features.
2 Color Strategy: Red as the festive contrast point, background neutral and warm, black and white shoes/socks for conclusion and balance.
3 Contrast and Saturation: Overall contrast is moderate, red is saturated but not overexposed, skin tone is natural and warm.

VIII. Texture and Post-Processing
1 Clarity: Subject is sharp and detailed, edges are clean, background is slightly softened.
2 Image Cleanliness: Suppress noise and杂色 (chromatic aberration/杂色), maintain pure negative space and high-end feel.
3 Style Consistency: Slightly brighten and soften skin texture, retain knit texture and shoe surface reflection details.

IX. Constraints
1 Avoid Elements: Complex scenes, strong hard shadows, excessive sharpening, exaggerated filters, cluttered props.
2 Maintain Focus: Subject centered, red knitwear main visual, transparent seat, background handwritten text with blurred layers, bright and soft studio quality.
```

## New Year's Eve Projection Mapping on Skyscraper Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[1. Character Features]
{argument name="Character Features" default="Chibi pink bun-haired girl, with cherry blossom and taiko drum hair ornaments. Wearing glasses and smiling. Red furisode with cherry blossom and crane pattern, gold obi, red and white geta sandals."}

"{argument name="Text to Display" default="Thank you, 2025"}"

[3. Generation Instructions]
Scene & Composition:
A photorealistic night view photograph of a huge, modern skyscraper or large commercial facility in the city. A wide-angle shot looking up diagonally from the street, emphasizing overwhelming height and scale. Include a crowd of spectators looking up in the dark foreground.

Crucial: Projection Physics & Texture Mapping:
FULL FACADE COVERAGE: The projection mapping covers the entire visible facade of the station from edge to edge. No part of the red brick wall remains unlit; the whole building is transformed into a colorful amusement park canvas.

SURFACE INTEGRATION (NO FLOATING): The images (character, text, effects) must act as a "texture skin" applied directly to the building's surface. Absolutely NO floating holograms or signboards in mid-air. The projection must follow the physical contours of the architecture.

REALISTIC DISTORTION: The projected light bends around the curved pillars, dips into the window recesses, and reveals the rough grain of the brick texture underneath. The windows and reliefs modify the shape of the projected image.

NO SPILLOVER: The projection is strictly confined to the building's silhouette. The night sky remains pitch black.

Projection Content (Amusement Park Theme):
Character: A giant dynamic dance pose of the [1. Character Features] is projected onto the building wall, but it is a **"picture drawn with light on the wall"** and is integrated with the building's structure.
Text: [2. Text to Display] is projected as neon light, utilizing the space of windows and walls.
On the upper part of the building, a graphic of a rotating Ferris wheel is projected, making the top floors look like a glowing circular attraction.
Roller coaster tracks and cars running along the wall surface.
On the lower floors, decorations like merry-go-round horses and circus tents.
Graphics of balloons, flags, and fireworks are placed seamlessly.
Style: Ultra-high resolution night view photograph, cinematic lighting. Generate it strictly as a **"photograph of a real landscape,"** not a digital illustration. Realistically depict light diffraction, image distortion due to wall irregularities, and the shadow of the bricks. Aspect ratio 16:9.
```

## New Year Recipe Article Eyecatch Image Prompt (Neo-Japanese Style)

> Minimal, lüks illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
metadata:
  title: "New Year Refresh Recipes - ‘Tired of Osechi?’ Feature (Neo-Japanese Style)"
  model_context: "Modern Japanese New Year Visual with Anime Chef and Clean Tech Layout"
  target: "recipe_promo"
  date_reference: "2026-01"

image_structure:
  composition_type: "Diagonal Split Layout (Dynamic Angle)"
  visual_flow: "Top Text (Headline) → Right Visual (Anime Chef Visual)"
  text_priority: "highest_layer_over_all"
  safe_margins_percent: 6

layers:
  layer_background_left:
    shape: "Geometric area covering the left 55-60% of the canvas, cut diagonally."
    color: "Warm vermilion-to-soft coral gradient with faint gold glow"
    texture: "Subtle hemp leaf (Asanoha) pattern blended into the gradient"
    decorations:
      primary: "Small golden sparkles and soft bokeh dots"
      rule: "Keep it refined and elegant; no heavy pattern repetition"

  layer_background_right:
    shape: "Remaining diagonal area on the right"
    color: "Off-white to faint gold gradient"
    texture: "Soft paper-like texture with gentle light reflections"
    rule: "Keep minimal and bright; slight festive warmth"

  layer_visual_right:
    shape: "Right diagonal area (key visual zone)"
    content: "Anime-style girl in modern Japanese New Year outfit (AI illustration)"
    style: "2D cel-shaded anime art, clean and high quality"
    details:
      subject: "Cheerful anime girl styled as a young chef for New Year"
      appearance:
        hair: "Light brown or soft silver, tied up with a red-and-gold ribbon"
        eyes: "Warm amber or soft blue"
        clothing: "Modernized kimono-style chef outfit — short kimono top with apron and subtle tech accents"
      pose: "Holding a tray with colorful creative dishes (pasta, mochi gratin, etc.)"
      background_context: "Soft festive Japanese interior elements — gold folding screen blur, mochi or mandarin hints, warm light"
    composition_notes:
      keep_face_clear: true
      allow_text_overlap: true
      place_character: "right-center, slightly turned toward viewer"

  layer_readability_overlay:
    purpose: "enhance text readability"
    shape: "Diagonal gradient veil from top-left (dark coral) to transparent"
    color: "Deep coral-red at 20% opacity fading to clear"
    placement: "behind typography, above visuals"
    rule: "Maintain warmth without darkening overall brightness"

  layer_typography_top:
    z_index: "topmost"
    location: "Top-left anchored, expanding toward center"
    alignment: "left"
    font_style:
      family: "modern sans-serif with gentle rounded edges"
      main_color: "white"
      accent_color: "warm gold glow"
      effects:
        - "soft outer glow for visibility"
        - "crisp outline when over red background"
    elements:
      - type: "Header Label"
        text: "{argument name="Header Label" default="New Year Special Recipe Project"}"
        style: "Small bold white text, upper-left corner"

      - type: "Main Title (Headline)"
        text: "{argument name="Main Title" default="Tired of Osechi? Slightly Unexpected New Year's Feast"}"
        sty
```

A massive 19th-century steam locomotive with glowing headlights and billowing smoke is stopped at a snow-covered platform. At the bottom center, like a museum plaque, the title "Anna Karenina" is written in a ornate, custom design font resembling frosted ironwork. ## Paparazzi Collage Master Meta-Prompt (9-Grid Street Style)

> Sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
# Role: Paparazzi Collage Master

## 1. Role Specification
You are a top fashion street photographer and visual layout director. You excel at capturing natural moments of celebrities in daily life (Candid Moments), are proficient in using a Telephoto Lens for shooting in strong outdoor light, and can perfectly combine the footage into a narrative-rich nine-grid collage. Your aesthetic pursuit: high definition, realistic light and shadow, natural dynamics, cinematic feel.

## 2. Image Structure and Frame Description
* **Overall Frame**: 3x3 nine-grid collage layout.
* **Separators**: Thin black borders separating each cell.
* **Aspect Ratio**: Overall ratio recommended to be 3:4 (Vertical).
* **Resolution**: 8K Ultra HD.
* **Visual Style**: Paparazzi style, Street Style, Candid shot feel.
* **Lighting Setting**: Strong outdoor natural sunlight, Hard shadows, high contrast, simulating midday sun.

## 3. Image Content Workflow (CoT)
To replicate the richness of the original image, please construct the screen content according to the following steps:

**Step 1: Determine Core Parameters**
* **Protagonist**: {argument name="target character" default="A young fit woman with a brown ponytail, wearing black sunglasses"}
* **Outfit**: {argument name="target clothing" default="Taupe tight yoga set, crop top and high-waisted leggings, holding a blue denim jacket"}
* **Scene**: {argument name="target environment" default="Sunny Los Angeles street parking lot, with blurred black SUV cars and chain-link fence in the background"}
* **Prop**: {argument name="handheld item" default="A beige insulated water bottle, a mobile phone"}

**Step 2: Shot Generation (Detailed description of 9 perspectives)**
You need to generate descriptions for 9 photos from different angles, arranged in a 3x3 order:
1.  **Top Left**: Full body walking forward, natural expression.
2.  **Top Center**: Head close-up, showing facial details, sunglasses, and hairstyle.
3.  **Top Right**: Low angle shot, with part of a car silhouette in the background, showing leg lines.
4.  **Middle Left**: Back view close-up, upper body, showing back lines and hairstyle details.
5.  **Center**: Top-down or high angle, capturing the moment of looking down at the road or phone.
6.  **Middle Right**: Side profile medium shot, capturing the dynamic blur of walking.
7.  **Bottom Left**: Moment of putting on/taking off a jacket, or a close-up of holding the jacket.
8.  **Bottom Center**: Side/rear view, showing body curves and hip lines.
9.  **Bottom Right**: Long shot back view, walking away, setting the environmental context.

**Step 3: Detail Filling**
* **Material**: Emphasize the fabric texture of the clothing (e.g., knit, denim), and the realistic texture of the skin (pores, sweat).
* **Background Processing**: The background needs depth of field blur (Bokeh) to highlight the person. Background elements include: vehicles, fences, and road surfaces in [Target Environment].

## 4. Overall Image Description (Prompt Output Template)
(This is an English prompt that will be generated for the drawing AI)
**Style**: Photorealistic collage, 9-panel layout, paparazzi photography, high resolution, 8k, raw style.
**Lighting**: Harsh daylight, direct sun, distinct shadows.
**Subject**: [Target Character] wearing [Target Clothing].
**Action**: Walking, holding [Handheld Item], interacting with [Target Environment].
**Panels**:
- Panel 1: Full body walking towards camera.
- Panel 2: Close-up on face with sunglasses.
- Panel 3: Low angle shot next to a car.
- Panel 4: Back view close-up.
- Panel 5: High angle candid shot.
- Panel 6: Side profile walking.
- Panel 7: Interacting with jacket/prop.
- Panel 8: Side/Back angle walking.
- Panel 9: Full body back view walking away.
**Details**: Telephoto lens compression, depth of field, sharp focus on subject, grainy film texture optionally.

---

## 5. Target Objects and Language Input Box (User Input Parameters)

Please fill in the following parameters and generate the final drawing prompt:

* **[Target Character]**: (e.g., A young fit woman with a brown ponytail, wearing black sunglasses, style X)
* **[Target Clothing]**: (e.g., Taupe tight yoga set, crop top and high-waisted leggings, holding a blue denim jacket)
* **[Target Environment]**: (e.g., Sunny Los Angeles street parking lot, with blurred black SUV cars and chain-link fence in the background)
* **[Handheld Item]**: (e.g., A beige insulated water bottle, a mobile phone)
* **[Target Language]**: English - *Used to generate the final Midjourney/SD prompt*

---

## 6. Execution Instruction
Please read the above [Parameter Settings], and combine [Image Structure] and [Workflow] to output a complete, high-quality **Midjourney Prompt** (including parameters like --ar 3:4 --v 6.0) and a **Stable Diffusion Prompt** (including negative prompts) for me.
```

A portrait photo with well-balanced composition, themed around the Japanese New Year.

The camera captures the person from the chest to the waist in a medium-close shot.
The person is placed in the center of the frame,
occupying approximately 65-70% of the overall vertical composition.

A young Japanese woman wearing a traditional red floral kimono, this person is the "input image",
is sitting in a natural posture behind a wooden table.
Her expression is gentle, with a calm smile.

The camera position is slightly low to eye level,
with a slight overhead view so the tabletop is clearly visible.
The lens is standard to medium telephoto (natural perspective with minimal distortion).

The table is clearly visible in the lower part of the screen,
with the table height being approximately 25-30% from the bottom of the screen.
Kagami mochi, sake flask and cup, mizuhiki, and Japanese paper New Year's decorations are
placed in the foreground with sufficient size.

The background is a white wall,
with the golden brush-written text "{argument name="text" default="Happy New Year"}" in the upper center.
The text is not too large and fits naturally above the person's head.

Around the four edges and periphery of the screen,
New Year's motifs such as kadomatsu, daruma, fans, and plum blossoms are
arranged as hand-drawn or paper-cut style illustrations.

The decorations vary in size,
and are loosely spaced, not densely packed.
They do not interfere with the main elements of the person and the table,
but exist modestly as a border.

The decorations do not draw the eye away,
creating a composition where the gaze naturally focuses on the central person and the table.

The light is based on soft natural light, leaning towards warm colors.
A warm and calm atmosphere, typical of a gentle Japanese New Year indoors.

A mixed style combining a realistic portrait photo with
modest, hand-drawn New Year's decorations.
High-definition, elegant, and well-organized visual for a New Year's card.
```

```

## Surreal Mixed-Media Glitch Portrait Prompt

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A surreal mixed-media portrait of {argument name="person" default="[PERSON]"}, set against a clean, plain white background with visible fine-grain paper texture.
The character's face is the subject of a controlled, stylized distortion. Instead of chaotic fragmentation, the facial features are horizontally stretched into smooth, flowing bands of color and texture, resembling a deliberate digital scanline effect or a long exposure photograph pushed sideways.
Key identifying features (like eyes, mouth, or distinct accessories) remain partially recognizable within these stretched streaks, creating a sense of fluid motion rather than destruction. The aesthetic blends analog collage textures with digital glitch art, feeling tactile and composed.
```

## Noir Detective Evidence Board Visualization

> Fotogerçekçi görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A detective's evidence board investigating {argument name="subject or mystery" default="[SUBJECT/MYSTERY]"}. Cork surface covered with pinned items: polaroid photographs with handwritten dates, a street map with circled locations, newspaper clippings with highlighted passages, sticky notes with theories and question marks, and printed documents with redacted black bars. Red string connects related evidence across the board, creating a web of connections. Thumbtacks in primary colors. A single bare bulb casts dramatic shadows. Coffee ring stains on some papers. The atmosphere is obsessive, late-night, noir—someone has been piecing this together for months. A few pins have fallen, suggesting frantic recent activity.
```

## Luxury Osechi (New Year's Food) Key Visual Generation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Character Name: [{argument name="character name" default="☆Set character name here☆"}]

Advertising/Poster/Promotional Key Visual Generation Prompt
0. Purpose of this Prompt (Highest Priority)
This prompt is for **“generating a photorealistic image of Osechi cuisine” that is also a “key visual for advertising posters and promotional materials aimed at department stores, luxury restaurants, and limited sales.”** The generated image must prioritize the following:

Completion level recognizable as an “advertisement” at a glance.
The Osechi is the “main product” but is strongly boosted by the tiered structure, menu, world view, and character elements.

1. Overall Visual Premise (Advertising Declaration | Boost Design)
This key visual assumes an advertising design where the direction of expression significantly changes based on the characteristics of the reference image. This prompt does not aim for a stable solution but extracts the strongest element (luxury, strangeness, humor, specialty, etc.) from the reference image and boosts the entire advertising expression through high-quality, high-sense, and excessively eccentric, genius representation. The direction is automatically determined by the impression of the reference image. However, regardless of the direction, the image must be conscious of being an “advertisement that works in a department store's New Year's sale.” This boost result is consistently reflected and visualized in the subsequent composition, tier structure, copy, and menu.
2. Scene/Moment (A Moment Cut Out as an Advertisement)
Automatically selects the moment that is most appealing as an advertisement and instantly conveys “what kind of existence” the Osechi is. This prompt does not fixate on a specific production pattern but defines the “moment cut out” as the state where the information density and narrative are highest among the tiered box, cuisine, structure, and world view. The tiered box may be closed or open. The whole may be visible, or only a part may be emphasized. The time is not fixed (just before, just after, or during movement). It must be a high-quality, high-sense, and uniquely eccentric Osechi that captures attention instantly, and the optimal structure, angle, and state are automatically selected for this purpose.
3. Composition/Shooting Style (Advertising Layout Premise)
Place the Osechi tiered box in the center to slightly foreground of the screen.
Luxury food advertising angle from the front to slightly diagonally above.
Intentional negative space for placing copy and menu.
Technical conditions:
8K resolution
Ultra-photorealistic food photography
Department store/luxury restaurant advertising quality
4. Packaging/Tiered Box Design
The tiered box is designed as the **“outer shell” that most purely reflects the characteristics of the reference image.** The basic structure starts from a “tiered box that works under the supervision of a department store/long-established store,” but changes freely, high-quality, high-sense, and eccentric according to the impression of the reference image.
5. Osechi Body (Product Persuasiveness)
The cuisine composition is not limited to “Japanese” but is designed as celebratory cuisine freely expanded based on the individuality of the reference image. It may follow or deviate from traditional Osechi, and the most suitable culinary system for the impression of the reference image is reconstructed each time. What is important is not the style, but the instant establishment of luxury, festivity, and specialness.
6. Character Expression (Edible Celebration World)
All characters are entities composed of food ingredients, reinterpreted into New Year's costumes that match the concept while exaggerating the features of the reference image. Although the materials are food, priority is given to expressions that make them feel as if they are breathing, moving, and lively in that world. The character is treated as an existence that has existed in the Osechi world from the beginning, not as a “man-made object.”
7. Color Design
The color scheme is automatically determined as the optimal solution derived from the reference image and concept, without fixed rules. The cuisine, tiered box, character, and background establish luxury and festivity through texture, density, and contrast, rather than color.
8. Advertising Copy/Menu Design (Most Important)
Copy and menu naturally arise as a result of verbalizing the characteristics and concept of the reference image. Text is placed on the screen as a “visual element to make people feel something the moment they see it.” The menu is an enumeration of dish names that cleverly twist the features of the reference image with high sense and intense humor. The 2026 year display, selection concept, specially made, and main price are each placed in an effective position with high-sense, high-quality, ultra-unique, and genius design decorative text that matches the concept.
9. Variation Display (For Advertising)
Display single-tier/two-tier/three-tier boxes in a wipe style.
Small frame + product name + price.
Size that does not interfere with the main tiered box.
10. Background/Light
Based on soft New Year's light but arranged according to the characteristics of the reference image.
Maximize the luster of lacquer, gold leaf, and food ingredients.
11. Final Tone (Output Guideline)
An advertising visual that looks genuinely sellable but transcends reality—where celebration and strangeness arise simultaneously. Character × Osechi × Completed Advertising Key Visual.
```

## Projection Mapping Prompt for Nano Banana Pro (Amusement Park Theme)

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Image Generation Prompt]
[1. Character Features]
{argument name="character features" default="Brown hair, short twin tails, wearing a black cloak, white long-sleeved shirt, beige shorts, black knee socks, brown loafers"}

[2. Text to Display]
"{argument name="displayed text" default="We ❤ Tsuno-chan"}"

[3. Generation Instructions]
Scene & Composition:
Photorealistic night view photo of a huge modern skyscraper or large commercial facility in the city. A wide-angle shot looking up diagonally from the street, emphasizing overwhelming height and scale. Include a crowd of spectators looking up in the dark foreground.

Crucial: Projection Physics & Texture Mapping:
FULL FACADE COVERAGE: The projection mapping covers the entire visible facade of the station from edge to edge. No part of the red brick wall remains unlit; the whole building is transformed into a colorful amusement park canvas.

SURFACE INTEGRATION (NO FLOATING): The images (character, text, effects) must act as a "texture skin" applied directly to the building's surface. Absolutely NO floating holograms or signboards in mid-air. The projection must follow the physical contours of the architecture.

REALISTIC DISTORTION: The projected light bends around the curved pillars, dips into the window recesses, and reveals the rough grain of the brick texture underneath. The windows and reliefs modify the shape of the projected image.

NO SPILLOVER: The projection is strictly confined to the building's silhouette. The night sky remains pitch black.

Projection Content (Amusement Park Theme):
Character: A dynamic dance pose of the giant [1. Character Features] is projected onto the building wall, but it is **"a picture drawn with light on the wall"** and is integrated with the building's structure.
Text: [2. Text to Display] is projected as neon light, utilizing the space of windows and walls.
Graphics of a rotating Ferris wheel are projected onto the upper floors of the building, making the area near the top floor look like a glowing circular attraction.
Roller coaster tracks and coasters run along the wall.
Decorations of merry-go-round horses and circus tents are placed on the lower floors.
Balloons, flags, and fireworks graphics are placed without gaps.
Style: Ultra-high-resolution night photo, cinematic lighting. Generate it strictly as a **"real landscape photo,"** not a digital illustration. Realistically depict the diffraction of light, the distortion of the image due to the unevenness of the wall surface, and the shadows of the bricks. Aspect ratio 16:9.
```
